`ء و ب` B001 in 88:25 names return to a return-place. **Cue:** the subject turns away in 88:23, but two ayat later the destination is fronted as `إِلَيْنَا`. **Role:** the attainment branch supplies the implied aim of self-directed withdrawal; return removes control over the final endpoint. **Abduction:** I infer an attempted autonomous goal from bodily turning; no goal is stated. **Reading change:** `تولى` can be read as failed escape: changing direction does not entail possessing the destination.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001307/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0f97fd670a15c6ec5cdc","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:23:4\",\"root_001307\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İnkârın nimeti örtmek anlamına da gelmesi, onu soyut bir düşünceden çıkarıp kişinin yaşadığı örtü, yüzey ve geçim düzeninin kaynağını tanımama haline getirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001307/B005","root_001684/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1e8ea54dd4e153a81aea","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:23:3\",\"root_001684\",[\"B004\"]],[\"88:23:4\",\"root_001307\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Bağlılık ve destekten çekilme ile açıkça ilişki kesme çağrışımları, kişinin hatırlatandan uzaklaşmasını anlatır; ancak bu kopuş ilahi yargı yetkisini ortadan kaldırmaz.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_24a79bc8d345cc78e298","text":"88:24 showed that relational severance leads to consequence without restoring messenger-control. 88:25 supplied the decisive reversal: the one who changes orientation still returns to a fixed destination; 88:26 then confirmed that disavowal does not remove the rejecter from account.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001307/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_385c9dcf649f1d4ad24e","text":"2. **Rejection also covers over received benefit.** **Lexical:** `ك ف ر` B004, \"covering over a favor.\" **Cue:** the visible world has just been presented as raised cover, fixed masses, and usable surface, and 88:21 converts those gifts into reminder. **Role:** the favor branch treats created habitability as something received, while rejection prevents its source from being acknowledged. **Abduction:** I infer benefaction from functional arrangement; 88:18-20 do not explicitly call the objects favors. **Reading change:** `كَفَرَ` becomes ingratitude embedded in material dependence, not abstract denial detached from the world inhabited.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001307/B004","root_001525/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_47980f3634ac0c02bae7","text":"3. **Covering favor within a provision surah.** Evidence: `ك ف ر` B004 covering over a favor/ingratitude; earlier `ن ع م` B001 favor, `س قي` water, `ج ر ي` provision, created signs. Structural cue: abundant provision and created order precede the refusal. Role: `كفر` is not only doctrinal rejection; it covers visible favor. Abductive move: signs and provisions impose gratitude unless covered over. Reading change: the ayah reads as ingratitude toward the very provision system displayed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001684/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_54f71ab2ff5850f37037","text":"2. **Mis-turned face in a surah of faces.** Evidence: `و ل ي` B006 turning face/attention, B007 turning away; `و ج ه` B001 faces in 88:2 and 88:8; `ن ظ ر` B001 looking in 88:17. Structural cue: the surah shows faces, commands looking, then names one who turns away. Role: `تولى` is a failed orientation of face/attention. Abductive move: accountability concerns what one faces after being shown signs. Reading change: the ayah reads spatially: refusal is misorientation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5bee7bd8d5546d101b16","text":"The repeated face/look orientation made `تولى` much more embodied: a face that should look turns away. The provision chain made `كفر` B004 especially active alongside B003.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001307/B003","root_001684/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_61e1c320962623b710db","text":"1. **Turning away and covering truth.** Evidence: `و ل ي` B007 turning away/withdrawal; `ك ف ر` B003 covering/rejecting truth. Structural cue: this follows the limit on control and precedes punishment by Allah. Role: the exception identifies the responsive failure: withdrawal plus concealment/rejection. Abductive move: once reminder is non-coercive, refusal must be named as the hearer’s turn. Reading change: the ayah reads as agency of rejection, not coercive capture.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001307/B001","root_001307/B003","root_001684/B006","root_001684/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7e062e0b2dca8bcc85e2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:23:3\",\"root_001684\",[\"B006\",\"B007\"]],[\"88:23:4\",\"root_001307\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yüz çevirme, daha önce bakışa açılan kanıtlardan dikkati bilinçli olarak başka yöne döndürür; inkâr da görülmüş gerçeğin üstünü örter.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001307/B005","root_001684/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a9a887bfddaa9458e7b6","text":"4. **Turning away and disavowal jointly sever relation without transferring jurisdiction.** **Lexical:** `و ل ي` B004, loyal alliance and support, negated by B007 withdrawal; `ك ف ر` B005, disavowal. **Cue:** 88:22 has already denied the messenger control, and 88:24 assigns punishment to God. **Role:** the paired verbs describe the rejecter's relational severance, while the surrounding authority sequence prevents that severance from becoming independence from judgment. **Abduction:** I infer loss of alliance from turning away plus disavowal. **Reading change:** the ayah distinguishes freedom from messenger-control from freedom from consequence: the former is affirmed, the latter is not.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001307/B001","root_001307/B003","root_001684/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ad34764511d985f1f916","text":"1. **Turning away is a deliberate reorientation of attention from exposed evidence.** **Lexical:** `و ل ي` B007, \"turning away and withdrawal,\" activated against B006, \"turning toward and facing\"; `ك ف ر` B001, covering and concealment, and B003, covering or rejecting truth. **Cue:** directed sight toward sky, mountains, and earth in 88:18-20 and reminder in 88:21 precede the paired verbs `تَوَلَّىٰ وَكَفَرَ`. **Role:** `تولى` reverses the earlier orientation of face, hearing, and attention; `كفر` then covers what that orientation had made visible. **Abduction:** I infer that the objects of rejection are the displayed constructions and reminder. **Reading change:** the two verbs form a perceptual mechanism: first avert the faculties, then conceal or deny the evidence from which they were averted.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001684/B012"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_adc68f37ec9435ea09e8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:23:3\",\"root_001684\",[\"B012\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yönelmenin bir hedefe varıp onu ele geçirmeyi de çağrıştırması, yüz çevirmeyi bağımsız bir varış denemesi gibi gösterir; iki ayet sonraki dönüş bu yönsel özerklik girişimini boşa çıkarır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only. Against that scale, the focus person's covering is no longer a stable hiding place but a small counter-cover located inside an arriving, comprehensive cover.","model_id":"d_counter_cover_of_overwhelming","reader_inference":"The packet supplies arrival, renewed report, and comprehensive envelopment; I supply the counter-cover arrow by making the human concealment fail inside the larger cover. A live alternative is that the opening only names the event and does not answer the focus act image-for-image.","status":"revised","structural_cues":["The surah opens with an arrival question and an enveloping event, while the focus clause near the close describes a human act of covering."],"trigger_roots":["ء ت ي","ح د ث","غ ش و"]},"qualification":{"anchor_scope_complete":true,"authoring_effect":"Source qualifications affect epistemic status and containment, never visibility or presumptive outcome.","packet_scope_relation":"broader_than_declared_pericope","reader_identity_status":"legacy_unbound","record_warnings":[]},"role":"hft_nomination_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_hft_a:d_counter_cover_of_overwhelming","source_type":"hft","support_id":"sup_f9c58c122ac9ab86e0a5","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[{"arabic_uthmani":"هَلْ أَتَىٰكَ حَدِيثُ ٱلْغَٰشِيَةِ","ayah_ref":"88:1"},{"arabic_uthmani":"فِى جَنَّةٍ عَالِيَةٍۢ","ayah_ref":"88:10"},{"arabic_uthmani":"إِلَّا مَن تَوَلَّىٰ وَكَفَرَ","ayah_ref":"88:23"}],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
